[EVENT: SYR116522]

Country: Syria | Location: As-Sweida

Actor1: Protesters (Syria) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Facebook; SHAAM; SOHR; Suwayda24; SY24 [Other-New media]

DESCRIPTION:
On 20 August 2023, hundreds of residents including As Sweida tribal Bedouin militia, Jabal Al Arab clan militia and rural As Sweida residents held a demonstration at a square in As-Sweida city calling for the implementation of UN resolution 2254, for the fall of the regime and improvement in living conditions, after tribal and clan militia members announced they would join the strike and protest. A general strike was also observed in the community, with attendees closing all shops and roads leading to the city in addition to the closure of government institutions in the community. University examinations were also postponed due to the general strike.
